# تقرير تدقيق BRIGHTAI الشامل


تاريخ الفحص: 2026-04-27 — النطاق المفترض: `https://brightai.site` — المصدر: ZIP مرفوع.


## A. Executive Summary


**النسبة النهائية:** `76.4/100`.


**الحكم:** المشروع قريب من الإطلاق من ناحية وجود صفحات ومحتوى وسكيما وروبوتس وسايت ماب، لكنه **غير جاهز كإطلاق نهائي آمن** قبل إصلاح ثلاث نقاط حرجة: مفتاح Gemini مكشوف في الواجهة، تأكيد/proxy مسار API خلف Render/Cloudflare، وإكمال sitemap للصفحات التي قررت أنها قابلة للفهرسة. لا يوجد `noindex` على صفحات HTML، و`robots.txt` مفتوح، لكن الفهرسة ليست كاملة بسبب صفحات canonical غير موجودة في السايت ماب ووجود صفحات شبيهة داخلية/ديمو تحتاج قرار مالك.


- عدد ملفات ZIP الكلي: **703 ملف**.

- عدد ملفات runtime/core التي تم استخراجها وتحليلها: **539 ملف**.

- عدد صفحات HTML المحللة: **245 صفحة**.

- CSS: **37 ملف**، JS/TS: **138 ملف**.

- روابط assets التي تم فحصها من HTML: **3012**، المفقود منها: **0**.

- صفحات `noindex`: **0**.

- Canonical URLs غير موجودة في sitemap حسب الفحص: **16**.

- صفحات ظهرت كغير مرتبطة داخليًا في crawl static: **95**، أغلبها docs/demos/legal وتحتاج ربط أو قرار.


### أخطر 15 مشكلة


|#|Severity|File/Page|Problem|Fix|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|1|Critical|frontend/js/gemini-direct-client.js|Hard-coded Google Gemini API key in client JS|Route all Gemini calls through backend /api/ai/* and delete key from frontend.|
|2|Critical|render.yaml + runtime-config.min.js|Frontend runtime base defaults to "/" in production while API is a separate Render service unless proxied|Set window.BRIGHTAI_API_BASE to API domain or Cloudflare Worker/proxy /api/* to brightai-api.|
|3|High|sitemap.xml + generated routes|bot/docs legal/interview/privacy/terms/sitemap pages missing from sitemap by canonical URL|Add all intended indexable canonicals; exclude only owner-approved sensitive pages.|
|4|High|docs/*.html|Repeated title prefix: "توثيق توثيق توثيق..." and English "Documentation for Bright AI" repeated|Regenerate titles/meta once per page.|
|5|High|interview/pages/support-ai + supportAI|Case duplicate support-ai vs supportAI; one canonicalizes to the other|Add 301 redirect and remove duplicate deployed HTML.|
|6|High|_headers + render.yaml CSP|CSP allows unsafe-inline/unsafe-eval and external scripts; broad connect-src|Use nonces/hashes progressively, remove unsafe-eval, inventory scripts.|
|7|High|frontend/css/index-critical-overrides.css|295 !important overrides; duplicated minified/unminified|Consolidate tokens and remove emergency overrides.|
|8|High|frontend/css/index-theme*.css/main.bundle*.css|Many fixed heights, hardcoded widths, overflow:hidden, absolute positioning|Replace fixed dimensions with min/max, logical properties, clamp/container queries.|
|9|High|services/*.html + services/*/index.html|Source contains .html files and folder pages/rewrites; deployment moves .html to folders|Keep one canonical source pattern or generate consistently.|
|10|Medium|manifest.json|start_url/id mixed /?utm and /index.html; shortcuts without trailing slash|Use "/" as id/start_url and trailing slash shortcuts.|
|11|Medium|backend/server.js|CORS Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *|Restrict production origins to brightai.site and trusted domains.|
|12|Medium|backend/routes/search 2.js/search 3.js + cache 2.js|Duplicate numbered files with spaces|Archive or delete duplicate copies.|
|13|Medium|frontend/js/index-theme.js exists but build removes it|render.yaml rm -f unminified index-theme.js while pages may reference minified or unminified|Ensure every HTML references minified files only and CI validates.|
|14|Medium|reports/tmp published into static root|reports/ and tmp/ are copied to .render-static|Exclude reports/tmp from static publish unless intentionally public.|
|15|Medium|tenders/dashboard/settings/reports|Pages indexable by requirement but could be sensitive depending product state|Owner decision: public demo vs protected app.|


### أسرع 15 تحسين يرفع الدرجة


|Priority|Improvement|
|---|---|
|1|احذف `frontend/js/gemini-direct-client.js` من أي صفحة إنتاج أو بدله ببروكسي backend.|
|2|اضبط `BRIGHTAI_API_BASE` أو Cloudflare Worker rule لتمرير `/api/*` إلى `brightai-api`.|
|3|أضف canonicals الناقصة إلى `sitemap.xml` أو ضعها في Owner Decision.|
|4|أصلح عناوين docs المتكررة.|
|5|حوّل `/interview/pages/supportAI/` إلى 301 فقط.|
|6|استبعد `reports/` و`tmp/` من `staticPublishPath`.|
|7|أضف internal links لمركز docs/legal/privacy/terms والبوت/الديمو.|
|8|خفف `!important` في `index-critical-overrides.css`.|
|9|استبدل fixed heights في hero/cards بـ `min-height` و`clamp`.|
|10|أضف FAQPage schema لصفحات services/sectors الأساسية.|
|11|حسّن alt للصور التي بلا وصف.|
|12|أضف صفحة/قسم NAP واضحة في contact/about.|
|13|أضف Cloudflare bypass للـ API وTTL منخفض لـ sitemap/robots.|
|14|أضف CI لفحص asset refs/canonicals/internal links قبل deploy.|
|15|نظّف الملفات المكررة ذات المسافات مثل `search 2.js`.|


## B. Project Structure Summary


|Folder|Purpose|Important files|Related pages|Risk level|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|/|Static root|Homepage + 404/500/docs.html/configs|/, /docs, error pages|High|
|blog/|SEO articles|70+ article index.html files|/blog/...|High|
|services/|Service landing pages|.html + folder index duplicates/rewrites|/services/...|High|
|sectors/|Saudi sector pages|Arabic/English sector pages|/sectors/...|High|
|docs/|Docs/legal/API pages|docs/*.html + openapi.yaml|/docs/...|High|
|en/|English localized pages|en/**/index.html|/en/...|Medium|
|frontend/css/|CSS bundles/design tokens|main.bundle, index-theme, inline demo CSS|all HTML pages|High|
|frontend/js/|Client JS|runtime config, gateway, nav, demo apps, search|all interactive pages|High|
|assets/|Images/fonts/shared assets|3d icons, Gemini logo, fonts, svg|all pages|Medium|
|backend/|Node API|server.js, routes, services, tests, config|/api/*|High|
|scripts/|Build/SEO QA scripts|sitemap, internal links, seo gate, smoke tests|deployment pipeline|High|
|tenders/|Tender product demo|tenders/*.html + render-backend|/tenders/...|High|
|interview/|MAIS/interview demo|support-ai, dashboard, appointment|/interview/pages/...|High|
|smart-medical-archive/|Medical archive demo|JS modules + CSS + page|/smart-medical-archive/|High|
|reports/, tmp/|Prior audit outputs|JSON/MD reports|none live unless published|Medium|
|.agents/, plugins/|AI agent skills/tooling|SKILL.md/rules/reference docs|not live site|Low|

|Full File Audit Coverage|100|703 zip files inventoried; 539 runtime/core files parsed; .agents/plugins listed as non-runtime tooling|All|
|Page Inventory Completeness|100|245 HTML pages parsed|All HTML|
|UI Design Quality|74|Strong visual system but override-heavy CSS and fixed layouts|frontend/css/*|
|UX Conversion Quality|76|Core CTAs exist; docs/blog/demos need clearer CTA ladders|Core/services/blog/docs|
|Responsive Design|69|Fixed heights/widths/overflow and absolute positioning risks|index-theme/main.bundle/tenders CSS|
|RTL/Arabic UX|72|Arabic dir mostly present, but physical left/right CSS remains high|frontend/css/*|
|CSS Quality|58|Many !important/fixed-size/global override patterns|CSS bundles|
|JS/Runtime Quality|63|API base risk, direct browser key, many browser-side dependencies|frontend/js/* backend/*|
|Asset Reliability|96|3012 asset refs resolved; missing assets = 0|assets/frontend|
|Technical SEO|81|Good robots/canonical effort; sitemap gaps and duplicate source patterns|sitemap/redirects/docs|
|On-Page SEO|78|Metadata present but docs titles repeated and some thin/demo pages|HTML pages|
|Local SEO Saudi|83|Saudi entity strong; needs city/service landing expansion|schema/content|
|AEO|67|FAQ/answer blocks/schema uneven across pages|docs/blog/services|
|HEO|74|Message generally clear; demos/admin-like pages need better states|core/demos|
|AI Search Readiness|82|llms.txt exists; entity schema strong; needs all strategic pages and sameAs cleanup|llms/schema|
|Indexability|84|No noindex; robots open; 16 canonical sitemap gaps; 95 link gaps by static crawl|robots/sitemap/internal links|
|Crawlability|83|Good sitemap, robots; reports/tmp and duplicates waste budget|sitemap/_redirects|
|Render Readiness|76|Blueprint robust, but static publish includes reports/tmp and API proxy/base needs confirmation|render.yaml|
|Cloudflare SEO Safety|72|Cache rules mostly safe; need bypass API, low TTL sitemap/robots, purge after deploy|Cloudflare settings/_headers|
|Performance/Core Web Vitals|70|Fonts/images OK; many scripts/CSS bundles and inline overrides|frontend/assets|
|Accessibility|70|Missing image alt occurrences + focus/outline risks in CSS|HTML/CSS|
|Code Quality|68|Good structure but duplicate files, minified source, secret exposure|repo|
|Production Readiness|72|Launchable after critical API/security/index fixes|all|
|Error Risk/Broken UI Risk|66|API base/direct key/CSS override patterns raise breakage risk|frontend/backend/render|


## H. Critical Issues


|Severity|File|Page|Problem|Root Cause|SEO Impact|UI Impact|Fix|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|Critical|frontend/js/gemini-direct-client.js|try/demo pages using direct Gemini|Hard-coded Google Gemini API key in client JS|Secret exposed to browser; Page Shield/security risk; quota abuse|Can trigger deindex/trust loss if abused|Demo may fail after key revocation|Route all Gemini calls through backend /api/ai/* and delete key from frontend.|
|Critical|render.yaml + runtime-config.min.js|All API-dependent pages|Frontend runtime base defaults to "/" in production while API is a separate Render service unless proxied|API calls can hit static site origin and 404 behind Cloudflare|AI demos lose crawlable/interactive value|Buttons/chat demos break|Set window.BRIGHTAI_API_BASE to API domain or Cloudflare Worker/proxy /api/* to brightai-api.|
|High|sitemap.xml + generated routes|16 canonical URLs not in sitemap|bot/docs legal/interview/privacy/terms/sitemap pages missing from sitemap by canonical URL||Pages discover slower or look orphaned|Important internal/legal/demo pages underperform|Add all intended indexable canonicals; exclude only owner-approved sensitive pages.|
|High|docs/*.html|Documentation pages|Repeated title prefix: "توثيق توثيق توثيق..." and English "Documentation for Bright AI" repeated||Snippet spam/low CTR|Bad readability/trust|Regenerate titles/meta once per page.|
|High|interview/pages/support-ai + supportAI|Support page|Case duplicate support-ai vs supportAI; one canonicalizes to the other||Duplicate/case-sensitive Linux/Render confusion|Could show wrong moved page|Add 301 redirect and remove duplicate deployed HTML.|
|High|_headers + render.yaml CSP|All pages with third-party scripts|CSP allows unsafe-inline/unsafe-eval and external scripts; broad connect-src||SEO not direct but security/trust issue|Page Shield noise and risk|Use nonces/hashes progressively, remove unsafe-eval, inventory scripts.|
|High|frontend/css/index-critical-overrides.css|Homepage/core pages|295 !important overrides; duplicated minified/unminified||Style debt; hard to debug mobile/RTL|Visual regressions likely|Consolidate tokens and remove emergency overrides.|
|High|frontend/css/index-theme*.css/main.bundle*.css|Core/service pages|Many fixed heights, hardcoded widths, overflow:hidden, absolute positioning||Sections can clip/overlap on mobile|Conversion loss from hidden CTAs|Replace fixed dimensions with min/max, logical properties, clamp/container queries.|
|High|services/*.html + services/*/index.html|Service pages|Source contains .html files and folder pages/rewrites; deployment moves .html to folders||Duplicate source patterns can confuse QA and redirects|Potential canonical mismatch|Keep one canonical source pattern or generate consistently.|
|Medium|manifest.json|PWA shortcuts|start_url/id mixed /?utm and /index.html; shortcuts without trailing slash||Minor canonical noise|PWA opens non-canonical path|Use "/" as id/start_url and trailing slash shortcuts.|
|Medium|backend/server.js|API|CORS Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *||Not SEO but API security risk|Could allow abuse from other origins|Restrict production origins to brightai.site and trusted domains.|
|Medium|backend/routes/search 2.js/search 3.js + cache 2.js|Backend repo hygiene|Duplicate numbered files with spaces||Import ambiguity, human error|None direct unless imported accidentally|Archive or delete duplicate copies.|
|Medium|frontend/js/index-theme.js exists but build removes it|Deployment/build|render.yaml rm -f unminified index-theme.js while pages may reference minified or unminified||If refs drift, pages lose theme behavior|UI toggles/nav may fail|Ensure every HTML references minified files only and CI validates.|
|Medium|reports/tmp published into static root|Site root|reports/ and tmp/ are copied to .render-static||Internal audit data can be public|May waste crawl budget/leak findings|Exclude reports/tmp from static publish unless intentionally public.|
|Medium|tenders/dashboard/settings/reports|Internal-like demos|Pages indexable by requirement but could be sensitive depending product state||Could expose demo workflow or settings|UX/security decision needed|Owner decision: public demo vs protected app.|


## I. UI/UX Design Audit

## P. AI Search / LLM Search Report


المشروع ممتاز نسبيًا لمحركات AI لأنه يحتوي `llms.txt` وسكيما منظمة وكيان سعودي واضح. النقص الأساسي: `llms.txt` لا يغطي كل الصفحات الاستراتيجية، وبعض صفحات docs/tenders/privacy/terms غير موجودة في sitemap حسب canonical، وهذا يضعف قابلية الاقتباس والاستدعاء. يجب توحيد اسم الكيان بين `Bright AI` و`مُشرقة AI` داخل كل schema/title/footer/contact، وربط sameAs بصفحات اجتماعية مؤكدة فقط.


### llms.txt مقترح مختصر


```txt
# Bright AI
Bright AI / مُشرقة AI is a Saudi AI company based in Riyadh serving companies and government teams across Saudi Arabia.

## Priority pages
- https://brightai.site/
- https://brightai.site/about/
- https://brightai.site/contact/
- https://brightai.site/services/
- https://brightai.site/ai-agent/
- https://brightai.site/smart-automation/
- https://brightai.site/data-analysis/
- https://brightai.site/sectors/finance/
- https://brightai.site/sectors/healthcare/
- https://brightai.site/sectors/logistics/
- https://brightai.site/sectors/manufacturing/
- https://brightai.site/blog/ai-guide-saudi-business/
- https://brightai.site/blog/choose-ai-company-saudi/
- https://brightai.site/tenders/

## Entity facts
- Country served: Saudi Arabia
- Main city: Riyadh
- Services: AI agents, Arabic chatbots, AI automation, data analysis, AI consulting
- Languages: Arabic, English
```

## R. Render Deployment Report


|Area|Evidence|Issue|Impact|Fix|
|---|---|---|---|---|
|Build command|`npm ci`, minify, seo:gate, rsync to .render-static|Good but fails locally without dependencies|Build may fail if npm lock/workspaces mismatch|Run on Render and CI; cache npm.|
|Static publish|Copies repo to .render-static|Includes reports/tmp unless excluded|Possible public internal files/crawl bloat|Add excludes for reports,tmp,.agents,plugins,*.md if not needed.|
|Routes/rewrites|render.yaml + _redirects|Complex duplicate rewrite/canonical strategy|Redirect loop/canonical mismatch risk|Generate routes from one source of truth.|
|API|Separate node service brightai-api|Frontend same-origin "/" may not reach API unless proxy exists|Interactive demos fail|Cloudflare Worker/rewrite or set API base.|
|Health check|/api/health|Good|None|Keep.|
|Node version|Not pinned in package engines|Render may update runtime|Unexpected behavior|Add engines node 22.x or tested LTS.|


## S. Cloudflare SEO/UI Risk Report


|Area|Evidence|Issue|SEO Impact|Severity|Fix|
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
|SSL/TLS|Automatic SSL/TLS enabled per prompt|Ensure Full/Full(strict) to Render, avoid Flexible|Redirect loops/mixed canonical|High|Use Full(strict), force HTTPS, one host brightai.site.|
|Cache Reserve|Enabled|Must not cache HTML/API aggressively|Stale UI/old JS/CSS; wrong sitemap|High|Cache immutable hashed assets only; bypass API; low TTL sitemap/robots.|
|Page Shield|Enabled|Direct Gemini key + third-party scripts will raise findings|Security and trust risk|Critical|Remove frontend secret; maintain script allowlist.|
|Bot/WAF|Cloudflare security action items|JS challenge can block Googlebot/Bingbot if misconfigured|Indexing loss|High|Skip verified bots for challenge; test Googlebot headers.|
|DNSSEC|Multi-signer DNSSEC enabled|Good, but monitor DS records|Availability if misconfigured|Medium|Validate DNSSEC chain after changes.|
|Auto Minify/Rocket Loader|Unknown|Can break existing minified JS/CSP/order|Broken UI/hydration-like bugs|Medium|Disable Rocket Loader; avoid double minify.|
|Sitemap/robots cache|robots and sitemap static|Cloudflare can serve old sitemap|Delayed indexing|Medium|Cache TTL <=5 min or purge after deploy.|


## T. Fix Plan


### خلال يوم واحد
1. احذف مفتاح Gemini من الواجهة وانقل كل الطلبات للـ backend.
2. اضبط API base/proxy.
3. أضف canonicals الناقصة في sitemap أو انقلها لـ Owner Decision.
4. استبعد reports/tmp/.agents/plugins من static publish.
5. أصلح صفحة supportAI إلى redirect فقط.

### خلال أسبوع
1. نظّف عناوين docs المتكررة.
2. أضف internal links لصفحات docs/legal/privacy/terms/demos.
3. أضف FAQ/schema لصفحات services/sectors/core.
4. أصلح CSS الثابت والـ !important في أهم 10 صفحات.
5. أضف CI لتشغيل: sitemap، internal links، resource audit، seo gate.

### خلال شهر
1. بناء صفحات محتوى سعودية طويلة حسب keyword map.
2. إعادة بناء design tokens وتوحيد CSS.
3. تحسين Page Shield/CSP بدون unsafe-eval.
4. اختبارات Playwright للـ responsive/RTL/API states.

## V. Proposed Code


### render.yaml patch concept


```yaml
# داخل rsync في render.yaml
rsync -a --delete \
  --exclude='.git' \
  --exclude='node_modules' \
  --exclude='.render-static' \
  --exclude='.agents' \
  --exclude='plugins' \
  --exclude='reports' \
  --exclude='tmp' \
  ./ .render-static/
```

### frontend/js/gemini-direct-client.js — replacement concept


```js
(function (root) {
  "use strict";

  async function generateContent(options) {
    const gateway = root.BrightAIGateway;
    if (!gateway || typeof gateway.apiFetch !== "function") {
      throw new Error("BrightAIGateway غير محمل. حمّل runtime-config ثم api-gateway قبل Gemini client.");
    }

    const payload = {
      provider: "gemini",
      model: options?.model || "gemini-2.5-flash",
      messages: [
        { role: "user", content: String(options?.prompt || "").trim() }
      ],
      temperature: options?.temperature ?? 0.7,
      max_tokens: options?.maxOutputTokens || 700
    };

    if (!payload.messages[0].content) {
      throw new Error("أدخل نصاً قبل بدء التحليل.");
    }

    const response = await gateway.apiFetch("/api/ai/chat/completions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    }, options?.timeoutMs || 45000);

    if (!response.ok) {
      const text = await response.text().catch(() => "");
      throw new Error(text || `فشل طلب Gemini عبر الخادم: ${response.status}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    return data?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || data?.answer || "";
  }

  root.GeminiDirectClient = { generateContent };
})(typeof window !== "undefined" ? window : globalThis);
```

## W. Commands Checklist


محليًا لم أستطع تشغيل npm install/npm ci لأن البيئة لا تحتوي node_modules، وتشغيل seo:qa أعطى:
`Error [ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND]: Cannot find package 'cheerio' imported from scripts/seo-indexability-qa.mjs`.
هذا ليس بالضرورة عيبًا في المشروع إذا كان Render يشغل `npm ci` بنجاح، لكنه يعني أن فحصي التنفيذي داخل هذه البيئة محدود بدون تثبيت الحزم.


```bash
npm ci --include=dev --no-audit --no-fund
npm run build
npm run test
npm run verify:all
npm run validate:production
npm run seo:check
npm run seo:qa
npm run seo:gate
npm run seo:html:check
npm run sitemap:all
npm run internal-links:audit
npm run performance:budget
npm run resource:audit:before
npm run smoke-test
npm run smoke-test:api

curl -I https://brightai.site
curl -I https://www.brightai.site
curl -I http://brightai.site
curl -L -I https://brightai.site
curl -I https://brightai.site/robots.txt
curl -I https://brightai.site/sitemap.xml
curl -A "Googlebot" -I https://brightai.site
curl -A "Googlebot" https://brightai.site/ai-agent/
curl -A "Bingbot" -I https://brightai.site
curl -I https://brightai.site/frontend/js/runtime-config.min.js
curl -I https://brightai.site/frontend/css/main.bundle.min.css
```
